## 评估口径

正确：在列明口径和观察窗口内，主要可检验内容符合。趋势符合：已有持续客观证据支持预测方向，最终结果尚待验证。部分符合：具体分项部分成立、部分不符，或合理口径产生不同结果。错误：所评内容与已发生结果相反。暂无法判断：无法确认趋势或结果、条件不明、对象难测或证据仍不足；缺证据不能自动换成趋势符合或部分符合。

明确未来终点尚未到达时，可以评价已有趋势，并保留原期限；这类进展单列为趋势符合，不作终局正确。单次波动、未来预测数据或仅有因果解释不算趋势证据。真正的前提条件仍须确认成立，作者的因果解释不自动增加为额外触发门槛。无期限持续判断可评价已观察阶段，不能证明永久成立。出版年只是未找到发言日期时的研究起点代理，出版前已知事实不是新的预见力证据。

“不太可能”没有被转换为数值概率；低概率事件发生可记录为方向反例，不能据单次结果证明概率失准。官方声明证明立场或制度安排，不自动证明实际成效；同时出现的政策与结果不自动证明因果。

### 1. TXT 第 395—398 行 · 排除：非预测

记录：`txt-696322ceaa-000020341-000020553`

原文：The mental and spiritual advances
which, in your day, mind in the solar system has still to attempt, are
overwhelmingly more complex, more precarious and dangerous, than those
which have already been achieved.

时间窗口：隐含，从出版年 1930 到截止日 2026-11-15。原文未写期限；该窗口内方向发生即按此评分。

扩展观点（解释，非原文）：未来人类的精神发展比过去更复杂危险，缺乏可直接观察的比较标准。

### 80. TXT 第 1704—1710 行 · 排除：非预测

记录：`txt-696322ceaa-000101218-000101594`

原文：The second
century completed the process of crystallization into two systems,
political and mental. On the one hand was the wealthy and close-knit
American Continental Federation, with its poor relations, South Africa,
Australia, New Zealand, the bedridden remains of Western Europe, and
part of the soulless body that was Russia. On the other hand were Asia
and Africa.

时间窗口：隐含，从出版年 1930 到截止日 2026-11-15。原文未写期限；该窗口内方向发生即按此评分。

were at last able to find a tolerable resting-place at the mouth of one

### 441. TXT 第 6681—6685 行 · 排除：非预测

记录：`txt-696322ceaa-000414713-000415038`

原文：Again and again, then, at intervals of a few thousand or a few hundred
thousand years, man’s whim was imposed upon the fauna and flora of the
earth, and at length directed to the task of remaking man himself. Again
and again, through a diversity of causes, the effort collapsed, and the
species sank once more into chaos.

时间窗口：原文写出（at intervals of a few thousand or a few hundred
thousand years）。

developments of the ancient human stock was that which led, through a

swallows.

### 714. TXT 第 10249—10254 行 · 排除：非预测

记录：`txt-696322ceaa-000640979-000641372`

原文：It was expected that some day, when we should have perfected the
species, there would be no need to build up successive generations, no
need of children, no need of all this schooling. It was expected that
the community would then consist of adults only; and that they would be
immortal not merely potentially but in fact, yet also, of course,
perennially in the flower of young maturity.

记录：`txt-696322ceaa-000666950-000667179`

原文：After the End, events unknowable
will continue to happen during a period much longer than that which will

potentiality of life and of spiritual development.
